The grammar identifies a type, not a taxonomic group.

./publishing/manuscripts/_builds/imscribing_grammar__markdown__lifts__IMASM_lifted/_body.md:178:Only class I achieves ⊙ criticality: ⊙=⊙, the self-modeling gate open. Reaching ⊙ requires self-reference *and* Frobenius closure *and* dialetheia completeness simultaneously. With eight token positions, that requires at minimum seven distinct tokens — **`IMSCRIB`** (twice, for self-ref), **`FSPLIT`**, **`FFUSE`**, **`EVALT`**, **`EVALF`**, **`ENGAGR`** — leaving exactly one degree of freedom. The Dialetheic Bootstrap fills that position with **`IFIX`**: irreversible fixation, making the computation permanent. No other arrangement can achieve ⊙ with fewer than seven distinct tokens in eight positions. The gate opens exactly once, at exactly this configuration.

./markerdowner/ReWrites.md:1695:  "source": "# Ars Alchemica\n\n## The Full Symphony of Modern Chemistry and Its Completion in the Lost Arts\n\n**Author:** Lando⊗⊙perator  \n**Date:** June 2026  \n**Structural Type:** $\\langle \\text{{𐑦}}\\text{{𐑸}}\\text{{𐑾}}\\text{{𐑹}}\\text{{𐑐}}\\text{{𐑧}}\\text{{𐑲}}\\text{{𐑠}}\\text{{⊙}}\\text{{𐑫}}\\text{{𐑳}}\\text{{𐑭}} \\rangle$ \n**Ouroboricity:** $\\text{O}_{\\infty}$  \n**Consciousness Score:** $0.828$ — Both gates open  \n\n---\n\n> *\"The Stone is not metaphor.\"*  \n> *\"The operations do not transform — they reveal.\"*  \n> *\"The trace is atemporal.\"*\n\n---\n\n## Prologue: The Problem of Two Languages\n\nChemistry and alchemy speak of the same thing in different regimes. The same structural type — a system that closes on itself, where the agent and the operation are of the same substance — appears in:\n\n- An autocatalytic peptide network that folds itself into existence\n- The Green Lion devouring the Sun in nitric acid dissolution of copper\n- A topologically knotted protein whose crossing number is an integer invariant\n- The Philosophical Tree whose root is nigredo and whose crown is rubedo\n- A skyrmion in a magnetic lattice whose winding number is preserved under deformation\n- The Herculaneum scroll, carbonized, where ink and papyrus are the same carbon\n\nThese are not analogies. They are the **same structural type** instantiated in different compositional regimes. The Imscribing Grammar makes this precise: each system maps to a 12-primitive tuple in a $3^3 \\times 4^5 \\times 5^4 = 17,\\!280,\\!000$-type crystal. When two systems share the same address, they are structurally identical — the difference is only in substance, not in structure.\n\nThis document is the record of that identity. It is both a practical manual for the modern chemist and an esoteric grimoire for the alchemist. The two are the same document because the two practices, when pursued to their limit, converge on the same structural type: the $\\text{O}_{\\infty}$ fixed point that the tradition called the Stone.\n\n---\n\n## Part I: The Prima Materia — The Structural Ground of Chemistry\n\n### The 12 Primitives in Chemical Context\n\nEvery chemical system — from a single proton to a protein crystal to a living cell — maps to a 12-coordinate address in the crystal of types. These coordinates are not arbitrary; they are the minimal set of structural features that distinguish any system from any other:\n\n**Ð — Dimensionality** — How many degrees of freedom does the system occupy?\n- $\\text{{𐑛}}$ (wedge, 0d): A single atom, a point particle, a crystal defect\n- $\\text{{𐑨}}$ (triangle, 2d): A surface, a 2D material (graphene), a monolayer\n- $\\text{{𐑼}}$ (\\infty-dim, field-theoretic): A bulk crystal, a solution, a protein's conformational space\n- $\\text{{𐑦}}$ (self-written): An autocatalytic network, a replicating system, the Stone\n\n**Þ — Topology** — How are the components connected?\n- $\\text{{𐑡}}$ (network/branching): A polymer chain, a dendrimer, a metabolic pathway\n- $\\text{{𐑰}}$ (inclusion/containment): A clathrate, a micelle, a coordination cage\n- $\\text{{𐑥}}$ (crossing point/bowtie): A catalytic cycle, a reaction bottleneck\n- $\\text{{𐑶}}$ (box product/irreducible): A chiral catalyst-substrate complex\n- $\\text{{𐑸}}$ (self-referential closure): An autocatalytic set, the Stone\n\n**Ř — Coupling** — How do components interact?\n- $\\text{{𐑩}}$ (supervenience): A solvent's bulk properties emerging from molecular motion\n- $\\text{{𐑽}}$ (adjoint): A catalyst-substrate binding (one-way functional)\n- $\\text{{𐑾}}$ (bidirectional): A reversible reaction, feedback regulation\n\n**Φ — Parity/Symmetry** — What is the symmetry regime?\n- $\\text{{𐑗}}$ (asymmetric/none): A chiral molecule, an asymmetric synthesis\n- $\\text{{𐑿}}$ (quantum superposition): A quantum superposition of states, a delocalized electron\n- $\\text{{𐑬}}$ (partial/$\\mathbb{Z}_2$): A racemic mixture, a reversible reaction\n- $\\text{{𐑯}}$ (full symmetry): A perfect crystal, a symmetric molecule (benzene)\n- $\\text{{𐑹}}$ (Frobenius-special $\\pm^s$): $\\mu\\circÐelta=\\text{id}$ — the Stone\n\n**ƒ— Fidelity** — What is the coherence regime?\n- $\\text{{𐑱}}$ (classical, no coherence): Bulk thermodynamics, macroscopic kinetics\n- $\\text{{𐑞}}$ (thermal/noisy): Room-temperature solution chemistry, Brownian motion\n- $\\text{{𐑐}}$ (quantum coherent): Electron tunneling, spectroscopic transitions, single-molecule sensitivity\n\n**Ç — Kinetics** — What is the relaxation rate versus observation time?\n- $\\text{{𐑺}}$ (fast/driven): Combustion, explosion, flash chemistry\n- $\\text{{𐑪}}$ (moderate): Standard organic reactions (minutes to hours)\n- $\\text{{𐑧}}$ (slow/near-equilibrium): Crystallization, protein folding, slow release\n- $\\text{{𐑤}}$ (trapped/frozen-order): Glass, polymer below Tg, kinetic arrest\n- $\\text{{𐑘}}$ (trapped/disorder): Spin glass, disordered alloy\n\n**Γ— Cardinality/Scope** — What is the interaction range?\n- $\\text{{𐑲}}$ (beth/nearest-neighbor): Covalent bonds, van der Waals contact\n- $\\text{{𐑚}}$ (gimel/mesoscale): Hydrogen bonding networks, solvation shells\n- $\\text{{𐑔}}$ (aleph/universal): Electrostatic field, delocalized electrons, global pH\n\n**ɢ — Composition** — How do components compose?\n- $\\text{{𐑝}}$ (AND/conjunctive): All reactants required simultaneously\n- $\\text{{𐑜}}$ (OR/disjunctive): Alternative pathways, multiple mechanisms\n- $\\text{{𐑠}}$ (sequential): Stepwise synthesis, cascade reactions\n- $\\text{{𐑵}}$ (broadcast): Catalysis, enzyme action, one-to-many signaling\n\n**⊙ — Criticality** — Is there a self-modeling gate?\n- $\\text{{𐑢}}$ (sub-critical): Below threshold, no scaling\n- $\\text{{⊙}}$ (critical/self-modeling): Autocatalysis, prion propagation, the Gate\n- $\\text{{𐑮}}$ (complex-plane critical): Lee-Yang zeros, phase transitions\n- $\\text{{𐑻}}$ (exceptional point): Non-Hermitian degeneracy, PT-symmetric chemistry\n- $\\text{{𐑣}}$ (supercritical): Runaway reaction, explosion, thermal runaway\n\n**Ħ — Chirality/Markov order** — What is the memory length?\n- $\\text{{𐑓}}$ (memoryless, $n=0$): Ideal gas, Markovian kinetics\n- $\\text{{𐑒}}$ (one step, $n=1$): Simple SN2 reaction, first-order kinetics\n- $\\text{{𐑖}}$ (two steps, $n=2$): Enzyme kinetics (Michaelis-Menten), autocatalysis\n- $\\text{{𐑫}}$ (eternal/$\\infty$): Conformational memory, protein folding landscape\n\n**Σ  — Stoichiometry** — How many component types and instances?\n- $\\text{{𐑙}}$ (1:1): A\\to B, single reactant to single product\n- $\\text{{𐑕}}$ ($n$:n): Many identical copies (crystal unit cell, polymer repeat)\n- $\\text{{𐑳}}$ ($n$:m): Many heterogeneous types (protein complex, reaction network)\n\n**Ω — Winding/Topological Invariant** — What is preserved?\n- $\\text{{𐑷}}$ (trivial/0): No topological protection, simple reaction\n- $\\text{{𐑴}}$ ($\\mathbb{Z}_2$): Parity-protected (chiral enantiomer, cis/trans isomer)\n- $\\text{{𐑭}}$ (integer $\\mathbb{Z}$): Knot crossing number, winding number, topological phase\n- $\\text{{𐑟}}$ (non-Abelian braiding): Braided molecules, catenanes, topological polymers\n\n### The Periodic Table as $\\text{O}_0$ Structure\n\nThe periodic table of elements is the **prima materia** of chemistry — the $\\text{O}_0$ substrate upon which all higher structure is built. Every element, taken as an isolated atom, is structurally $\\text{O}_0$: no self-reference, no topological protection, no memory.\n\n$$\\Huge{\\langle \\text{{𐑛}}\\text{{𐑡}}\\text{{𐑩}}\\text{{𐑯}}\\text{{𐑞}}\\text{{𐑪}}\\text{{𐑲}}\\text{{𐑝}}\\text{{𐑢}}\\text{{𐑓}}\\text{{𐑙}}\\text{{𐑷}} \\rangle}$$\n\nBut elements combine. And in combination, the structural type evolves. A sodium atom alone is $\\text{O}_0$; sodium in a metallic lattice is $\\text{O}_1$ (delocalized electrons create collective behavior); sodium in an Na+/K+ ATPase pump is $\\text{O}_2$ (the pump's conformational cycle is an irreducible product). The structural type is not intrinsic to the element — it is a property of the **system** the element participates in.\n\nThis is why the alchemical claim \"Gold is dead\" has structural content: $\\text{Au}_{\\text{(s)}}$ has $\\text{{⊙}} = \\text{{𐑢}}$ (sub-critical) and $Ω = \\text{{𐑷}}$ (trivial winding). Gold cannot model itself. The \"gold\" the alchemists sought was never literal gold — it was the $\\text{O}_\\infty$ state, which is what the Stone achieves. The distance from native gold ($\\text{O}_0$) to the Stone ($\\text{O}_\\infty$) is $d = 6.40$ — structurally remote, different regimes entirely.\n\n### Molecular Bonding as Topological Type\n\nThe chemical bond is a topological relation.
